The Court has determined that tenders, such as the construction of sewers and infrastructure, are being used outside the scope of the tender provision. The Court's report stressed that all expenses made so far must be immediately taken back from those who have been handed over, and that the administrative responsibilities of executives who have assigned these vehicles to them must be sought out. The report found that the criteria for the Bank of Provinces to provide a vehicle under the tender are not clear and that the main owners of the work increase work-related costs unnecessarily. Mr. Secretary, our producers have their own very important and demands to be met. I would like to take this opportunity to convey these requests to you from our manufacturers' own language. The most important demands of our manufacturers are the height of the input costs of all our producers who fish for fresh water both in the sea and in the soil pools and in the rivers. Once in a while, when fish prices drop, our producers even have trouble getting food for their fish. In this regard, our producers expect your support. Again, KTV is not taken from the diesels of culture fishermen who produce in the sea, but it is administered in the diesel purchases of cultural fishermen who produce in soil pools. This injustice must be dealt with. It must be ensured that there is no TV from the diesel used by our fishmen who produce soil pools. Fishermen who produce soil pools are paid to use spring water. However, water used in these pools is already dull and salty water, not drinking and using water. Therefore, our fishermen must not be paid to use resource water. Mr. Secretary, again, the rate of VAT in the meat has been reduced to 1%. The VAT rate in fish is 8%. The reduction of the VAT rate in the fish to one percent will both eliminate unfair competition and allow our citizens to eat healthier. A good plan must be made so that tourists and the environment do not get hurt while the fish ponds at sea are being planned. Again, in Milas County -- which is our most important fishing district in the sea and in the pools -- the logistical support pier that our fishermen need is absolutely necessary in a suitable place between Gulluk and Kazaklı region. Since this pier is not built, from time to time, tourists and fishermen face each other. A very important part of the fish produced is exported abroad, especially in EU countries. It will also comfort our producers to reduce Turkish Airlines cargo prices in fish exports and to provide our exporter with ease in this regard. In this regard, all our producers await your support. Mr. Secretary, I look forward to your attention and support in this matter, and I salute your grand delegation.

Thus, this process is seen to be in harmony with past practices.
